> **Claim ceiling.** `implemented` means a reachable implementation was found and its evidence still exists in the tree. It is not `verified` (an acceptance check was run and recorded) and not `deployed` (seen working on a deployed environment). No row is `verified` or `deployed` yet, because no row has that evidence recorded; a row moves only when its evidence does. `proposed` means catalogued with no repo audit behind it; `specified` means audited and nothing built.
